[does_not_resolve] Appendix A bracket '[k-amendment, design-instance authored, PROPOSAL-k commit ⟨hash⟩]' vs §5 step 1 'same commit … hash filled by Code; hash = timestamp' vs status line 'The commit hash is this document's timestamp' — the 5(e) precedent
  at: PROPOSAL-k-echo-channel.md:534 (App A); :500-501 (§5 step 1); :7-8 (status); :113-115 (§0.3)
  proposal: App A: "(f) [k-amendment, design-instance authored, PROPOSAL-k commit ⟨hash⟩]"; §5: "Commit this document; 5(f) and 28(d) applied to CLAUDE.md on the same commit as annotations (hash filled by Code); hash = timestamp."; status: "The commit hash is this document's timestamp."; §0.3: "applied to CLAUDE.md as an annotation on THIS proposal's commit, the 5(e)/PROPOSAL-h pattern, with the commit hash Code fills"
  record: `git log -S'(e) [h-amendment' --oneline -- CLAUDE.md` → "6897b67 PROPOSAL-h batch: rule 5(e) amended verbatim, …" (one commit, not 34eb7ee). `git show 34eb7ee --stat` → "Proposal H and Addendum G1 — ADDENDUM-g1-archival-reading-screen.md | 54, PROPOSAL-h-state-present-choice.md | 195; 2 files changed" — no CLAUDE.md. CLAUDE.md:274-275 (written in 6897b67): "(e) [h-amendment, PROPOSAL-h §0.4, design-instance authored, applied on its commit `34eb7ee` 2026-08-20; text verbatim]". RESULTS.md:76-78 (the STOP entry): "5(e) (CLAUDE.md cites commit `34eb7ee`; the clause itself entered CLAUDE.md at `6897b67` — a caveat for the \"5(e) pattern\" 5(f) is to follow)".
  evidence: A git commit hash is the SHA-1 of the commit object, which covers the tree, so no file inside a commit can carry that commit's own hash. The precedent the proposal invokes did it in TWO commits: 34eb7ee (2026-08-20 16:56 -0400) committed PROPOSAL-h alone; 6897b67 (2026-08-20 21:07 UTC, eleven minutes later) applied 5(e) to CLAUDE.md naming the earlier proposal commit `34eb7ee`. §5 step 1's "on the same commit … (hash filled by Code)" cannot be executed as written; the "5(e)/PROPOSAL-h pattern" §0.3 cites is proposal-commit-first, annotation-commit-second. Code's STOP entry already flagged this caveat and v1.1 did not absorb it ("no design content changed", :7).
  note: Two further points. (1) The structural gate in scripts/k1_stage1.py:56,79 checks only `FIVE_F_MARKER = "(f) [k-amendment"` and that CLAUDE.md is tracked and clean — it would pass with ⟨hash⟩ left unfilled, so the gate does not enforce the fill. (2) "hash = timestamp" sits beside the proposal's own stamp discipline "Code stamps from `date -u`" (:16, :528) and the status line's "PI clock 18:21 EDT / 22:21 UTC" — the hash can serve as the document's external timestamp only if it is written somewhere OTHER than the document (the RESULTS entry, or the CLAUDE.md bracket in a following commit), which is the precedent's shape. Resolution options are the design seat's: two commits (proposal, then annotation naming it), or App A's bracket filled with the proposal's hash in a second commit the way 6897b67 named 34eb7ee.
